你在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/ 项目（工作目录 game/）。Terraria 复刻，1:1 对齐反编译源码 Terarria1456/（1.4.5.6）。

任务：补齐微光（Shimmer）液体的运行时语义 + aether 树。背景：世界生成侧 ShimmerPass 已 1:1 写入（liquidType 4、石壳椭圆腔体），但 (a) LiquidSim 沉降会把生成好的 800+ 格微光泄流到近 0（模拟器不认识 type 4 或把它当普通流体泄漏），(b) 渲染端 VanillaLiquidRenderer.toVanillaType 未映射 4（按水渲染）。

【A. 微光液体模拟（对照原版 Liquid.cs）】
1. 读 Terarria1456/Terraria/Liquid.cs 中 shimmer 相关全部语义（grep "shimmer"）：流动性（会不会流平？）、与水/岩浆相遇的转化（Shimmer ↔ 水 = 石化？原版：shimmer 接触 lava 生成 Aether 石块？接触水会怎样——核实）、delWater/calculateDelta 处理、PanicMode。
2. 读我们的 src/world/liquid/（LiquidSim 等，记忆：Liquid.cs 一比一重写过）与 src/world/gen/vanilla/ShimmerPass.ts（生成写入形态：腔体内壁、底部液体格数）。
3. 修：(a) LiquidSim 对 liquidType 4 的流动/转化语义按原版补齐——若原版 shimmer 完全不流动（静止液体）就标记静止不参与沉降；若流动则按原版规则（含与水的转化、转化产物 tile）；(b) 沉降入口（settleWorldLiquids gen/load 模式）对 4 的处理与原版一致。(c) 渲染：src/render/VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts 的 toVanillaType 加 4 的映射（微光贴图：terraria-assets 里找 liquid shimmer 贴图，grep 素材表；若无独立贴图用最接近原版观感的方式——半透明紫粉色+微光色，注释注明近似）。(d) TileStore.liquidType 注释补 4。
4. 存档链路确认：SaveFile/RLE 编解码对 liquidType 4 已天然支持（byte），抽查即可。

【B. Aether 树（TryGrowingTreeByType）】
1. 读原版 cs:35057 附近（ShimmerMakeBiome 内 TryGrowingTreeByType(583-589)）与树种植实现（Terraria.GameContent.Generation 或 WorldGen.TryGrowingTree）：aether 树 = Gem tree 族（584-589 是黄玉/紫晶/翡翠/蓝玉/钻石/红宝石树的 tile id？核实）——种在腔内石柱/地面上，长法与普通树差异。
2. 我们已有 TreePass（src/world/gen/vanilla/TreePass.ts）——读它的树种植机制（树干 tile/树高随机场），尽量复用其种植循环给 ShimmerPass 补 aether 树（宝石树：树干 tile 583+variety，树叶 gem 段），做不到 1:1 的部分（树生长模拟器级细节）做静态种植近似并注明。
3. 接到 ShimmerPass 里（cs:35057 同位置：石柱放置后种树）。

【验证】
- npx tsc --noEmit 零新增（遗留忽略：NpcDrops zoneSnow、tests/enemy-shooters、tests/p5-equip、Game.ts heldPrefixStat、stats/Buffs.ts、TileLightScanner）。
- /tmp tsx 脚本：生成 4200×1200 世界（任一 seed），统计 liquidType==4 的格数——生成后立即统计 vs 模拟 settleWorldLiquids('gen') 若干轮后再统计，修复前期望泄流到 0、修复后应保持稳定（若原版语义允许流平则按原版结果断言，如实报告数字）。
- npx vitest run tests/ 里 liquid 相关测试（ls tests/ 找 liquid/water 命名的）全绿；跑 scripts/_gen-micro-probe.mjs（生成探针）确认无回归。
- 浏览器渲染冒烟可跳过（vite dev 环境不稳），渲染改动以代码审查+typecheck 为准即可。

约束：可改 src/world/liquid/、src/world/gen/vanilla/ShimmerPass.ts、src/render/VanillaLiquidRenderer.ts、TileStore 注释、tests 之外的文件；禁止碰 src/world/spawn/、VanillaSpawner.ts（另一代理在改数据，虽然不同文件但避免误伤）。报告 ≤35 行：原版 shimmer 语义摘要（流动性/转化表）、改动清单、泄流前后数字、aether 树实现程度、近似点。

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T07:34:05.395Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Key vanilla semantics:

- Shimmer IS a flowing liquid: in `Update()`, shimmer tiles flow just like water (no delay like lava/honey). `tile5.shimmer()` → `ShimmerCheck(x,y)`, and neighbors with shimmer cause AddWater. So shimmer flows at same speed as water.
- `SettleWaterAt` (QuickWater during gen): flag2 = shimmer; `if (WorldGen.isGeneratingOrLoadingWorld && flag4 && !flag && !flag2)` — the waterLine→lava conversion is skipped for honey and shimmer, so shimmer settles downward/flows as its own type (b preserved). Shimmer participates in QuickWater settling normally (it's moved as its own liquid type b=3), then `AttemptToMoveShimmer` runs ShimmerCheck on adjacent differing types.
- Merge table: shimmer + anything (water/lava/honey) → Aether Block tile 659 (Aetherium? actually 659 = "Aether" block... In 1.4.4 Aether Block? Hmm — 659 is Aetherium? Let me check: 658 = "Aether Block"? Let me check TileID. 659 seems to be the aether stone block used by ShimmerMakeBiome? Need to verify). GetLiquidMergeTypes: thisLiquidType=3 (shimmer) + waterNearby → tile 659; + lavaNearby → 659; + honey → 659. And when thisLiquidType is water/lava/honey and shimmerNearby → tile 659, liquidMergeType=3. So any contact → Aether Block (659), resulting liquid becomes shimmer (the remaining liquid type is 3).

Wait actually liquidMergeType=3 means "the merge liquid type is shimmer"? In LiquidCheck, the liquidMergeType is passed to CreateLiquidMergeTile → GetLiquidChangeType(thisLiquidType, liquidMergeType) — for sound/net. The mergeType semantics: which liquid "survives"? Actually in LiquidCheck, after summing neighbor liquids of different type, if num >= 24, tile5.liquid = 0 and CreateLiquidMergeTile at (x,y) places the tile. The neighbors' liquids were already zeroed. So the tile is placed at x,y (the this-liquid tile). Hmm — and liquidMergeType returned... it's used only for sound type in CreateLiquidMergeTile (GetLiquidChangeType). So visually: when two different liquids meet, a block is created.

Note important: `CreateLiquidMergeTile` during world gen (`isGeneratingOrLoadingWorld && !createMergeTilesDuringGen`): doesn't place tiles — instead does LiquidOverwriteStrip converting the whole strip to lava (liquidType=1) unless merge tile is 229/230/56/659 mapping. For 659, liquidType = 3 → LiquidOverwriteStrip(x,y,3) — converts whole row strip to shimmer! Interesting.

Also `WorldGen.ShimmerRemoveWater()` called in QuickWater when generating/loading — let me check that; it probably removes water near shimmer or fills? Let me grep.

Now let me look at our LiquidSim.ts.

	public static void ShimmerRemoveWater()
	{
		if (!generatingWorld)
		{
			return;
		}
		int num = 150;
		int num2 = (int)GenVars.shimmerPosition.X - num;
		int num3 = (int)GenVars.shimmerPosition.X + num;
		int num4 = (int)GenVars.shimmerPosition.Y - num;
		int num5 = (int)GenVars.shimmerPosition.Y + num / 2;
		for (int i = num4; i <= num5; i++)
		{
			for (int j = num2; j <= num3; j++)
			{
				if (InWorld(j, i) && (int)Math.Sqrt(Math.Pow(Math.Abs(j - (int)GenVars.shimmerPosition.X), 2.0) + Math.Pow(Math.Abs(i - (int)GenVars.shimmerPosition.Y), 2.0)) < num)
				{
					if (!Main.tile[j, i].shimmer())
					{
						Main.tile[j, i].liquid = 0;
					}
					if (Main.tile[j, i].type == 162)
					{
						Main.tile[j, i].active(active: false);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

155	  addWater(x: number, y: number) {

164	    }

187	  updateLiquid() {
188	    const st = this.world.store;
189	    const killThreshold = 8; // 单机 num（1456 Liquid.cs:995）
190	    // panic 模式（1456 Liquid.cs:1019-1070）：缓冲 ≥45000 持续 3600 次未解 →
191	    // 自底向上逐行 QuickWater 强制沉降，每次调用处理 5 行，到顶后 WaterCheck 退出
192	    if (!this.quickSettle) {
193	      if (!this.panicMode) {
194	        if (this.buffer.length >= 45000) {
195	          this.panicCounter++;
196	          if (this.panicCounter > 3600) {
197	            this.panicMode = true;
198	            this.panicCounter = 0;
199	            this.panicY = st.h - 3;
200	            this.liquids.length = 0;
201	            this.numLiquid = 0;
202	            this.buffer.length = 0;
203	          }
204	        } else {
205	          this.panicCounter = 0;
206	        }
207	      }
208	      if (this.panicMode) {
209	        let n = 0;
210	        while (this.panicY >= 3 && n < 5) {
211	          n++;
212	          this.quickWater(this.panicY, this.panicY);
213	          this.panicY--;
214	        }
215	        if (this.panicY < 3) {
216	          this.panicMode = false;
217	          this.panicCounter = 0;
218	          this.waterCheck();
219	        }
220	        return;
221	      }
222	    }
223	    const quickSettle = this.quickSettle;
224	    // quickFall 跟随 quickSettle（Liquid.cs:752-755，未受 gen 门限）；但 cycles=1 的
225	    // Main.cs:12251 分支被 !WorldGen.gen 限定——读档沉降期 gen=true 不生效，保持分片
226	    this.quickFall = quickSettle;
227	    const cycles = this.cycles;
228	    this.wetCounter++;
229	    const slice = Math.floor(this.curMaxLiquid / cycles);
230	    const start = slice * (this.wetCounter - 1);
231	    let end = slice * this.wetCounter;
232	    if (this.wetCounter === cycles) end = this.numLiquid;
233	    if (end > this.numLiquid) { end = this.numLiquid; this.wetCounter = cycles; }
234	    if (this.quickFall) {
235	      for (let l = start; l < end; l++) {
236	        const e = this.liquids[l];
237	        e.delay = 10;
238	        this.update(e);
239	        this.skip[this.idx(e.x, e.y)] = 0;
240	      }
241	    } else {
242	      for (let l = start; l < end; l++) {
243	        const e = this.liquids[l];
244	        const i = this.idx(e.x, e.y);
245	        if (!this.skip[i]) this.update(e);
246	        else this.skip[i] = 0;
247	      }
248	    }
249	    if (this.wetCounter >= cycles) {

252	      for (let l = this.numLiquid - 1; l >= 0; l--) {

256	          if (st.liquid[i] === 254) st.liquid[i] = 255;
257	          this.delWater(l);
258	        }
259	      }
260	      // LiquidBuffer 回灌：按剩余空位逐个重新入列（Liquid.cs:799-807）
261	      if (this.buffer.length > 0) {
262	        const free = Math.min(this.curMaxLiquid - this.numLiquid, this.buffer.length);
263	        for (let n = 0; n < free; n++) {
264	          const b = this.buffer.shift()!;
265	          this.checking[this.idx(b.x, b.y)] = 0;
266	          this.addWater(b.x, b.y);
267	        }
268	      }
269	      // 卡死检测：活动量长期不变 → 清空列表靠唤醒自愈（Liquid.cs:808-824）
270	      if (this.numLiquid > 0 && this.numLiquid > this.stuckAmount - 50 && this.numLiquid < this.stuckAmount + 50) {
271	        this.stuckCount++;
272	        if (this.stuckCount >= 10000) {
273	          this.stuck = true;
274	          for (let l = this.numLiquid - 1; l >= 0; l--) this.delWater(l);
275	          this.stuck = false;
276	          this.stuckCount = 0;
277	        }
278	      } else {
279	        this.stuckCount = 0;
280	        this.stuckAmount = this.numLiquid;
281	      }
282	    }
283	  }
284	
285	  // ================= 单格流程 Update（Liquid.cs:298-674） =================
286	
287	  private update(e: LiquidEntry) {
288	    const st = this.world.store;
289	    const w = st.w, h = st.h;
290	    const x = e.x, y = e.y;
291	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
292	    // 1) 本格被实心方块占据 → 下轮必删（Liquid.cs:306-310）
293	    if (this.blocksLiquid(x, y)) { e.kill = 999; return; }
294	    const startAmt = st.liquid[i5];
295	    // 2) 地狱蒸发：水每 tick -2（Liquid.cs:314-320；UnderworldLayer ≈ h-200）
296	    if (y > h - 200 && st.liquidType[i5] === 1 && st.liquid[i5] > 0) {
297	      st.liquid[i5] = Math.max(0, st.liquid[i5] - 2);
298	    }
299	    if (st.liquid[i5] === 0) { e.kill = 999; return; }
300	    const myType = st.liquidType[i5];
301	    // 3) 岩浆/蜂蜜：先交互检查，再降速（Liquid.cs:327-373）
302	    if (myType === 2) {
303	      this.lavaCheck(x, y);
304	      if (!this.quickFall) {
305	        if (e.delay < 5) { e.delay++; return; }
306	        e.delay = 0;
307	      }
308	    } else {
309	      // 水格：唤醒岩浆邻居，让对方自己的 Update 处理交互（Liquid.cs:342-349）。
310	      // 标量展开(2026-08 审计 G4):元组数组+迭代器在此热路径 ≈2.5M 对象/s
311	      for (let t = 0; t < 4; t++) {
312	        const nx = x + (t === 0 ? -1 : t === 1 ? 1 : 0);
313	        const ny = y + (t === 2 ? -1 : t === 3 ? 1 : 0);
314	        if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
315	        const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
316	        if (st.liquid[ni] > 0 && st.liquidType[ni] === 2) this.addWater(nx, ny);
317	      }
318	      if (myType === 3) {
319	        this.honeyCheck(x, y);
320	        if (!this.quickFall) {
321	          if (e.delay < 10) { e.delay++; return; }
322	          e.delay = 0;
323	        }
324	      } else {
325	        // 唤醒蜂蜜邻居（Liquid.cs:365-372）——标量展开,同上
326	        for (let t = 0; t < 4; t++) {
327	          const nx = x + (t === 0 ? -1 : t === 1 ? 1 : 0);
328	          const ny = y + (t === 2 ? -1 : t === 3 ? 1 : 0);
329	          if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue;
330	          const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
331	          if (st.liquid[ni] > 0 && st.liquidType[ni] === 3) this.addWater(nx, ny);
332	        }
333	      }
334	    }
335	    // 4) 向下全量下灌（Liquid.cs:375-397）
336	    {
337	      const bi = this.idx(x, y + 1);
338	      const belowAmt = st.liquid[bi];
339	      const belowType = st.liquidType[bi];
340	      const belowBlocks = y + 1 >= h ? true : this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1);
341	      if (!belowBlocks && (belowAmt <= 0 || belowType === myType) && belowAmt < 255) {
342	        let t = 255 - belowAmt;
343	        if (t > st.liquid[i5]) t = st.liquid[i5];
344	        // 原版边界特性：缺口 1 且本格满格时不扣源（Liquid.cs:381-384）
345	        const flag = t === 1 && st.liquid[i5] === 255;
346	        if (!flag) st.liquid[i5] -= t;
347	        st.liquid[bi] += t;
348	        st.liquidType[bi] = myType;
349	        this.addWater(x, y + 1);
350	        this.skip[bi] = 1;
351	        this.skip[i5] = 1;
352	        if (this.quickSettle && st.liquid[i5] > 250) st.liquid[i5] = 255;
353	        else if (!flag) { this.addWater(x - 1, y); this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
354	      }
355	    }
356	    // 5) 侧向均流（Liquid.cs:398-651）
357	    if (st.liquid[i5] > 0) this.sideFlow(x, y, i5);
358	    // 6) kill 维护（Liquid.cs:652-671）
359	    if (st.liquid[i5] !== startAmt) {
360	      if (st.liquid[i5] === 254 && startAmt === 255) {
361	        if (this.quickSettle) st.liquid[i5] = 255;
362	        e.kill++;
363	      } else {
364	        this.addWater(x, y - 1);
365	        e.kill = 0;
366	      }
367	    } else {
368	      e.kill++;
369	    }
370	  }
371	
372	  /** 侧向均流：参与格全体写平均值（Liquid.cs:398-651，逐分支照抄） */
373	  private sideFlow(x: number, y: number, i5: number) {
374	    const st = this.world.store;
375	    const myType = st.liquidType[i5];
376	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, im2 = i5 - 2, ip2 = i5 + 2, im3 = i5 - 3, ip3 = i5 + 3;
377	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir];
378	    // flag1 左可流 / flag2 右可流 / flag3 左 2 格延伸 / flag4 右 2 格延伸
379	    let f1 = true, f2 = true, f3 = true, f4 = true;
380	    if (this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y)) f1 = false;
381	    else if (lq > 0 && st.liquidType[il] !== myType) f1 = false;
382	    else if (this.blocksLiquid(x - 2, y)) f3 = false;
383	    else if (st.liquid[im2] === 0) f3 = false;
384	    else if (st.liquidType[im2] !== myType) f3 = false;
385	    if (this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y)) f2 = false;
386	    else if (rq > 0 && st.liquidType[ir] !== myType) f2 = false;
387	    else if (this.blocksLiquid(x + 2, y)) f4 = false;
388	    else if (st.liquid[ip2] === 0) f4 = false;
389	    else if (st.liquidType[ip2] !== myType) f4 = false;
390	    let num1 = 0;
391	    if (st.liquid[i5] < 3) num1 = -1;         // 薄层蒸发偏置（Liquid.cs:424-426）
392	    if (st.liquid[i5] > 250) { f3 = false; f4 = false; }
393	    if (f1 && f2) {
394	      if (f3 && f4) {
395	        // ±3 延伸判定（Liquid.cs:436-449）
396	        let f5 = true, f6 = true;
397	        if (this.blocksLiquid(x - 3, y)) f5 = false;
398	        else if (st.liquid[im3] === 0) f5 = false;
399	        else if (st.liquidType[im3] !== myType) f5 = false;
400	        if (this.blocksLiquid(x + 3, y)) f6 = false;
401	        else if (st.liquid[ip3] === 0) f6 = false;
402	        else if (st.liquidType[ip3] !== myType) f6 = false;
403	        if (f5 && f6) {
404	          // 7 格均分（Liquid.cs:452-515）
405	          const m = csRound((lq + rq + st.liquid[im2] + st.liquid[ip2] + st.liquid[im3] + st.liquid[ip3] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 7); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
406	          let same = 0;
407	          if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[il] = myType; same++; }
408	          if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; same++; }
409	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m) { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; st.liquid[im2] = m; this.addWater(x - 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; same++; }
410	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m) { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; same++; }
411	          if (st.liquid[im3] !== m) { st.liquidType[im3] = myType; st.liquid[im3] = m; this.addWater(x - 3, y); } else { st.liquidType[im3] = myType; same++; }
412	          if (st.liquid[ip3] !== m) { st.liquidType[ip3] = myType; st.liquid[ip3] = m; this.addWater(x + 3, y); } else { st.liquidType[ip3] = myType; same++; }
413	          // 补偿唤醒：中心可能保持原值，邻居需按"邻居≠均值或中心≠均值"重唤醒（Liquid.cs:502-513）
414	          if (st.liquid[il] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
415	          if (st.liquid[ir] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
416	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 2, y);
417	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 2, y);
418	          if (st.liquid[im3] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 3, y);
419	          if (st.liquid[ip3] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 3, y);
420	          // 中心例外：六邻全等于均值且上方有液 → 保持原值（Liquid.cs:514）
421	          if (same !== 6 || st.liquid[iu] <= 0) st.liquid[i5] = m;
422	        } else {
423	          // 5 格均分（Liquid.cs:519-562）
424	          const m = csRound((lq + rq + st.liquid[im2] + st.liquid[ip2] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 5); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
425	          let same = 0;
426	          if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[il] = myType; same++; }
427	          if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; same++; }
428	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m) { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; st.liquid[im2] = m; this.addWater(x - 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[im2] = myType; same++; }
429	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m) { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); } else { st.liquidType[ip2] = myType; same++; }
430	          // 补偿唤醒（Liquid.cs:553-560）
431	          if (st.liquid[il] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
432	          if (st.liquid[ir] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
433	          if (st.liquid[im2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x - 2, y);
434	          if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) this.addWater(x + 2, y);
435	          if (same !== 4 || st.liquid[iu] <= 0) st.liquid[i5] = m;
436	        }
437	      } else if (f3) {
438	        // 4 格：左延伸（Liquid.cs:567-587）。类型写在条件判断之前（原版 L568 无条件写）
439	        const m = csRound((lq + rq + st.liquid[im2] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 4); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
440	        st.liquidType[il] = myType;
441	        if (lq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); }
442	        st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
443	        if (rq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
444	        st.liquidType[im2] = myType;
445	        if (st.liquid[im2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[im2] = m; this.addWater(x - 2, y); }
446	        st.liquid[i5] = m;
447	      } else if (f4) {
448	        // 4 格：右延伸（Liquid.cs:590-609）。类型写在条件判断之前（原版 L591 无条件写）
449	        const m = csRound((lq + rq + st.liquid[ip2] + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 4); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
450	        st.liquidType[il] = myType;
451	        if (lq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); }
452	        st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
453	        if (rq !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); }
454	        st.liquidType[ip2] = myType;
455	        if (st.liquid[ip2] !== m || st.liquid[i5] !== m) { st.liquid[ip2] = m; this.addWater(x + 2, y); }
456	        st.liquid[i5] = m;
457	      } else {
458	        // 3 格均分（Liquid.cs:613-628）：随机 254→255 补满
459	        let m = csRound((lq + rq + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 3); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
460	        if (m === 254 && this.genRand() * 30 < 1) m = 255; // 原版 WorldGen.genRand.Next(30)==0（确定性）
461	        if (lq !== m) { st.liquidType[il] = myType; st.liquid[il] = m; this.addWater(x - 1, y); } else st.liquidType[il] = myType;
462	        if (rq !== m) { st.liquidType[ir] = myType; st.liquid[ir] = m; this.addWater(x + 1, y); } else st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
463	        st.liquid[i5] = m;
464	      }
465	    } else if (f1) {
466	      // 2 格：向左（Liquid.cs:633-639）
467	      const m = csRound((lq + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 2); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
468	      if (lq !== m) st.liquid[il] = m;
469	      st.liquidType[il] = myType;
470	      if (st.liquid[i5] !== m || st.liquid[il] !== m) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
471	      st.liquid[i5] = m;
472	    } else if (f2) {
473	      // 2 格：向右（Liquid.cs:643-649）
474	      const m = csRound((rq + st.liquid[i5] + num1) / 2); // 1456 Math.Round=取偶
475	      if (rq !== m) st.liquid[ir] = m;
476	      st.liquidType[ir] = myType;
477	      if (st.liquid[i5] !== m || st.liquid[ir] !== m) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
478	      st.liquid[i5] = m;
479	    }
480	  }
481	
482	  // ================= 液体交互（Liquid.cs:888-1115） =================
483	
484	  /** 岩浆格与水/蜂蜜接触 → 黑曜石 56 / 脆蜂蜜块 230（阈值 24） */
485	  private lavaCheck(x: number, y: number) {
486	    const st = this.world.store;
487	    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:898）
488	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
489	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;
490	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];
491	    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];
492	    if ((lq > 0 && lt !== 2) || (rq > 0 && rt !== 2) || (uq > 0 && ut !== 2)) {
493	      // 情形 A：左右/上方有非岩浆液体 → 生成在岩浆格（Liquid.cs:905-959）
494	      let sum = 0;
495	      let type = OBSIDIAN;
496	      if (lt !== 2) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }
497	      if (rt !== 2) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }
498	      if (ut !== 2) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }
499	      if (lt === 3 || rt === 3 || ut === 3) type = CRISPY_HONEY;
500	      if (sum < 24) return;
501	      const t = st.type[i5];
502	      if (t !== 0) {
503	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
504	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);  // 近似 tileObsidianKill
505	        else return;                             // 平台等保留方块 → 不生成
506	      }
507	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
508	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
509	      st.setTile(x, y, type);
510	    } else {
511	      if (dq <= 0 || dt === 2) return;
512	      // 情形 B：仅下方有水/蜂蜜 → 生成在下方格（Liquid.cs:961-1014）
513	      const belowT = st.type[id];
514	      if (belowT !== 0) {
515	        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];
516	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0); // tileCut / obsidianKill 近似
517	        else return;
518	      }
519	      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }
520	      const type = dt === 3 ? CRISPY_HONEY : OBSIDIAN;
521	      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
522	      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;
523	      st.setTile(x, y + 1, type);
524	    }
525	  }
526	
527	  /** 蜂蜜格与水接触 → 蜂蜜块 229（阈值 32） */
528	  private honeyCheck(x: number, y: number) {
529	    const st = this.world.store;
530	    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:1020）
531	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
532	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;
533	    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];
534	    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];
535	    if ((lq > 0 && lt === 1) || (rq > 0 && rt === 1) || (uq > 0 && ut === 1)) {
536	      let sum = 0;
537	      if (lt === 1) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }
538	      if (rt === 1) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }
539	      if (ut === 1) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }
540	      if (sum < 24) return; // 1456 LiquidCheck 统一阈值 24
541	      const t = st.type[i5];
542	      if (t !== 0) {
543	        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
544	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);
545	        else return;
546	      }
547	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
548	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
549	      st.setTile(x, y, HONEY_BLOCK);
550	    } else {
551	      if (dq <= 0 || dt !== 1) return;
552	      const belowT = st.type[id];
553	      if (belowT !== 0) {
554	        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];
555	        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0);
556	        else return;
557	      }
558	      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; } // 1456 统一 24
559	      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
560	      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;
561	      st.setTile(x, y + 1, HONEY_BLOCK);
562	    }
563	  }
564	
565	  // ================= DelWater（Liquid.cs:1117-1213） =================
566	
567	  private delWater(l: number) {
568	    const st = this.world.store;
569	    const e = this.liquids[l];
570	    const x = e.x, y = e.y;
571	    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);
572	    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, id = i5 + st.w;
573	    const amt = st.liquid[i5];
574	    if (amt < 2) {
575	      // 残量清零 + 邻居唤醒（Liquid.cs:1125-1137）
576	      st.liquid[i5] = 0;
577	      if (st.liquid[il] < 2) st.liquid[il] = 0; else this.addWater(x - 1, y);
578	      if (st.liquid[ir] < 2) st.liquid[ir] = 0; else this.addWater(x + 1, y);
579	    } else if (amt < 20) {
580	      // 水膜抹除：邻格更低或下方不满（Liquid.cs:1138-1142）
581	      const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], dq = st.liquid[id];
582	      if ((lq < amt && !this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y)) ||
583	          (rq < amt && !this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y)) ||
584	          (dq < 255 && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1))) {
585	        st.liquid[i5] = 0;
586	      }
587	    } else if (st.liquid[id] < 255 && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1) && !this.stuck && !this.blocksLiquid(x, y)) {
588	      e.kill = 0; // 下方未满：留在列表继续流（Liquid.cs:1143-1147）
589	      return;
590	    }
591	    if (st.liquid[i5] < 250 && st.liquid[i5 - st.w] > 0) this.addWater(x, y - 1);
592	    if (st.liquid[i5] === 0) {
593	      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;
594	    } else {
595	      const rq = st.liquid[ir], lq = st.liquid[il];
596	      if (rq > 0 && rq < 250 && !this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y) && st.liquid[i5] !== rq) this.addWater(x + 1, y);
597	      if (lq > 0 && lq < 250 && !this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y) && st.liquid[i5] !== lq) this.addWater(x - 1, y);
598	      if (st.liquidType[i5] === 2) {
599	        this.lavaCheck(x, y);
600	        // 岩浆 3×3 烧除（1456 DelWater L1557-1587）：草族(2/23/109/199/477/492)→空气；
601	        // 蘑菇草族(60/70/661/662)→泥土 59——经 sheet 反查内部 id 精确映射
602	        for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
603	          for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
604	            const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
605	            if (nx < 1 || ny < 1 || nx >= st.w - 1 || ny >= st.h - 1) continue;
606	            const ni = this.idx(nx, ny);
607	            const t = st.type[ni];
608	            if (t === 0) continue;
609	            if (this.lavaBurnAirIds.has(t)) st.setTile(nx, ny, 0);
610	            else if (this.lavaBurnDirtIds.has(t)) st.setTile(nx, ny, this.dirtId);
611	          }
612	        }
613	      } else if (st.liquidType[i5] === 3) {
614	        this.honeyCheck(x, y);
615	      }
616	    }
617	    // swap-remove（Liquid.cs:1193-1197）：只搬 x/y/kill，delay 保留本槽旧值（原版语义）
618	    this.numLiquid--;
619	    this.checking[i5] = 0;
620	    const tail = this.liquids[this.numLiquid];
621	    e.x = tail.x; e.y = tail.y; e.kill = tail.kill;
622	    this.liquids.length = this.numLiquid;
623	  }
624	
625	  // ================= 读档沉降：QuickWater（Liquid.cs:85-103 / 105-212） =================
626	
627	  /** 自底向上逐湿格直接搬运沉降（原版 QuickWater(verbose, -1, -1)：y 从 h-3 到 3） */
628	  quickWater(minY = 3, maxY = -1) {
629	    const st = this.world.store;
630	    const yMax = maxY < 0 ? st.h - 3 : maxY;
631	    for (let y = yMax; y >= minY; y--) {
632	      for (let x = 4; x < st.w - 4; x++) {
633	        if (st.liquid[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0) this.settleWaterAt(x, y);
634	      }
635	    }
636	  }
637	
638	  /** 单格液体直接搬到最终落点（Liquid.cs:105-212 逐行对照） */
639	  private settleWaterAt(originX: number, originY: number) {
640	    const st = this.world.store;
641	    const oi = this.idx(originX, originY);
642	    if (st.liquid[oi] === 0) return;
643	    let X = originX, Y = originY;
644	    const srcType = st.liquidType[oi];
645	    let liquid = st.liquid[oi];
646	    st.liquid[oi] = 0;
647	    let flag1 = true;
648	    for (;;) {
649	      // 1) 垂直下落：下方空且可通行就一直落（Liquid.cs:121-130）
650	      let flag2 = false;
651	      while (Y < st.h - 5 && st.liquid[this.idx(X, Y + 1)] === 0 && !this.blocksLiquid(X, Y + 1)) {
652	        Y++;
653	        flag2 = true;
654	        flag1 = false;
655	      }
656	      // （Liquid.cs:129-130 的 waterLine 岩浆转换仅世界生成期生效，读档跳过）
657	      // 2) 蛇形横向铺开（Liquid.cs:131-195）
658	      let dir = -1;          // num2：当前行走方向
659	      let step = 0;          // num3：当前行步数
660	      let lastDir = -1;      // num4：最后空位方向
661	      let lastStep = 0;      // num5：最后空位步数
662	      let hitL = false;      // flag4：左边界
663	      let hitR = false;      // flag3：右边界
664	      let dropped = false;   // flag5：本轮发生下落
665	      for (;;) {
666	        const probeX = X + step * dir;
667	        // 越界防护：原版 C# 越界会抛异常，JS 的 idx 会静默回绕到上一行——
668	        // 超出 [0, w-1] 的探测一律视为边界命中，不再读取
669	        if (probeX < 0 || probeX >= st.w) {
670	          if (dir === -1) hitL = true; else hitR = true;
671	          if (hitL && hitR) break;
672	          if (hitR) { dir = -1; step++; }
673	          else if (hitL) { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = 1; }
674	          else { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = -dir; }
675	          continue;
676	        }
677	        const ci = this.idx(probeX, Y);
678	        if (st.liquid[ci] === 0) { lastDir = dir; lastStep = step; }
679	        if (dir === -1 && probeX < 5) hitL = true;
680	        else if (dir === 1 && probeX > st.w - 5) hitR = true;
681	        const bi = this.idx(probeX, Y + 1);
682	        // 侧下方同类型未满 → 直接灌入（Liquid.cs:149-159）
683	        if (st.liquid[bi] !== 0 && st.liquid[bi] !== 255 && st.liquidType[bi] === srcType) {
684	          let t = 255 - st.liquid[bi];
685	          if (t > liquid) t = liquid;
686	          st.liquid[bi] += t;
687	          liquid -= t;
688	          if (liquid === 0) break;
689	        }
690	        if (Y >= st.h - 5 || st.liquid[bi] !== 0 || this.blocksLiquid(probeX, Y + 1)) {
691	          // 下方堵住 → 沿行继续 / 折返（Liquid.cs:162-191）
692	          const aheadX = X + (step + 1) * dir;
693	          const inRange = aheadX >= 0 && aheadX < st.w;
694	          const ni = inRange ? this.idx(aheadX, Y) : -1;
695	          const nAmt = ni >= 0 ? st.liquid[ni] : 0;
696	          const nSolid = inRange ? this.blocksLiquid(aheadX, Y) : true;
697	          if ((nAmt !== 0 && (!flag1 || dir !== 1)) || nSolid) {
698	            if (dir === 1) hitR = true; else hitL = true;
699	          }
700	          if (hitL && hitR) break;
701	          if (hitR) { dir = -1; step++; }
702	          else if (hitL) { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = 1; }
703	          else { if (dir === 1) step++; dir = -dir; }
704	        } else {
705	          dropped = true;
706	          break;
707	        }
708	      }
709	      // 落点写回钳制到有效列（防 lastDir/lastStep 记录异常时把液体写出世界）
710	      X = Math.max(2, Math.min(st.w - 3, X + lastStep * lastDir));
711	      if (liquid !== 0 && dropped) Y++;
712	      else break;
713	    }
714	    // 3) 写回落点（Liquid.cs:204-205）
715	    const fi = this.idx(X, Y);
716	    st.liquid[fi] = liquid;
717	    st.liquidType[fi] = srcType;
718	    if (st.liquid[fi] > 0) {
719	      // 落地即交互（Liquid.cs:206-210 / AttemptToMoveLava/Honey 248-280）
720	      this.attemptToMoveLiquid(X, Y, srcType, 2);
721	      this.attemptToMoveLiquid(X, Y, srcType, 3);
722	    }
723	  }
724	
725	  /** 落点与异种液体相邻时触发交互检查（Liquid.cs:248-280 AttemptToMoveLava 同构）。
726	   *  原版语义：邻居的 target 性 != 落点格的 target 性 才算"异种相遇"——
727	   *  水落水旁不算，水落岩浆旁才算（否则整片海洋互相当岩浆转黑曜石） */
728	  private attemptToMoveLiquid(X: number, Y: number, srcType: number, targetType: number) {
729	    const st = this.world.store;
730	    const srcIs = srcType === targetType;
731	    const check = targetType === 2 ? (x: number, y: number) => this.lavaCheck(x, y)
732	      : (x: number, y: number) => this.honeyCheck(x, y);
733	    const diff = (x: number, y: number) => {
734	      const i = this.idx(x, y);
735	      return st.liquid[i] > 0 && (st.liquidType[i] === targetType) !== srcIs;
736	    };
737	    if (diff(X - 1, Y)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X - 1, Y); }
738	    else if (diff(X + 1, Y)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X + 1, Y); }
739	    else if (diff(X, Y - 1)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X, Y - 1); }
740	    else if (diff(X, Y + 1)) { if (srcIs) check(X, Y); else check(X, Y + 1); }
741	  }
742	
743	  // ================= WaterCheck（WorldGen.cs:48955-49009） =================
744	
745	  /** 全图自底向上重建活动列表：能流的/液面不齐的/岩浆旁有异种的都入列 */
746	  waterCheck() {
747	    const st = this.world.store;
748	    this.liquids.length = 0;
749	    this.numLiquid = 0;
750	    this.buffer.length = 0; // 原版 WaterCheck 首行清 LiquidBuffer（WorldGen.cs:48959）
751	    this.checking.fill(0);
752	    for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
753	      for (let y = st.h - 2; y > 0; y--) {
754	        const i = this.idx(x, y);
755	        if (st.liquid[i] === 0) continue;
756	        const t = st.type[i];
757	        if (t !== 0) {
758	          const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
759	          if (d && d.solid && !d.platform) { st.liquid[i] = 0; continue; } // 实心格内液体清零（1456 无 379 系统特例）
760	          // 1456 WaterCheck L81172-81187：液体冲毁可死亡方块（走 KillTile）
761	          if (st.liquidType[i] === 2) { if (this.lavaDeathIds.has(t)) this.killTile?.(x, y); }
762	          else if (this.waterDeathIds.has(t)) this.killTile?.(x, y);
763	        }
764	        const id = i + st.w;
765	        const dq = st.liquid[id];
766	        if (!this.blocksLiquid(x, y + 1) && dq < 255) {
767	          if (dq > 250) st.liquid[id] = 255;
768	          else this.addWater(x, y);
769	        }
770	        const il = i - 1, ir = i + 1;
771	        if (!this.blocksLiquid(x - 1, y) && st.liquid[il] !== st.liquid[i]) this.addWater(x, y);
772	        else if (!this.blocksLiquid(x + 1, y) && st.liquid[ir] !== st.liquid[i]) this.addWater(x, y);
773	        if (st.liquidType[i] === 2) {
774	          const iu = i - st.w;
775	          if ((st.liquid[il] > 0 && st.liquidType[il] !== 2) ||
776	              (st.liquid[ir] > 0 && st.liquidType[ir] !== 2) ||
777	              (st.liquid[iu] > 0 && st.liquidType[iu] !== 2) ||
778	              (dq > 0 && st.liquidType[id] !== 2)) this.addWater(x, y);
779	        }
780	      }
781	    }
782	  }
783	}

117	    x1 = Math.trunc(X + n5 * n4) + rng.int(0, 15);

131	        }
132	        num16++;
133	        if (rng.int(0, 2) < num16) { num16 = 0; n18--; m += rng.int(-1, 1); }
134	        if (n19 <= 0) n18--;
135	        n19--;
136	        n17++;
137	      }
138	      const n = m + n18 - rng.int(1, 2); // 循环退出后 n == m+num18,再退 Next(1,3)
139	      if (st.inBounds(n, n17 - 2)) { const ti = st.idx(n, n17 - 2); st.type[ti] = STONE; st.flags[ti] = 1; }
140	      if (st.inBounds(n, n17 - 1)) { const ti = st.idx(n, n17 - 1); st.type[ti] = STONE; st.flags[ti] = 1; }
141	      if (st.inBounds(n, n17)) { const ti = st.idx(n, n17); st.type[ti] = STONE; st.flags[ti] = 1; }
142	      if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) {
143	        if (st.inBounds(n, n17 + 1)) { const ti = st.idx(n, n17 + 1); st.type[ti] = STONE; st.flags[ti] = 1; }
144	        placeTight(st, rng, n, n17 + 2);
145	      } else {

147	      }

151	  shimmerOpening(st, rng, -1, X - n5, Y, n8);

155	
156	/** cs:35095-35125 ShimmerMakeBiomeOpening */
157	function shimmerOpening(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, dir: number, X: number, Y: number, size: number): void {
158	  let num = X;
159	  let y = Y;
160	  size--;
161	  const solidAt = (x: number, yy: number) =>
162	    st.inBounds(x, yy) && st.flags[st.idx(x, yy)] !== 0;
163	  for (;;) {
164	    num += dir;
165	    let open = true;
166	    for (let i = y - size + 1; i < y - 1; i++) {
167	      if (solidAt(num, i)) open = false;
168	      if (solidAt(num + dir, i)) open = false;
169	      if (solidAt(num + dir * 2, i)) open = false;
170	      if (st.inBounds(num, i)) st.flags[st.idx(num, i)] = 0;
171	    }
172	    for (let j = y - size; j < y; j++) {
173	      if (st.inBounds(num - dir, j)) st.flags[st.idx(num - dir, j)] = 0;
174	    }
175	    if (solidAt(num - dir, y - size - 1) && st.inBounds(num - dir, y - size - 1)) st.wall[st.idx(num - dir, y - size - 1)] = 0;
176	    if (solidAt(num - dir, y) && st.inBounds(num - dir, y)) st.wall[st.idx(num - dir, y)] = 0;
177	    if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) y += rng.int(-1, 1);
178	    if (open || num < 50 || num > st.w - 50 || Math.abs(num - X) > 100) break;
179	  }
180	}
181	
182	/** cs:38329 PlaceTight 简化:石笋 165(PreferSmall 随机 1-2 格高,雪原小支不触发) */
183	function placeTight(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {
184	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) return;
185	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y - 1) || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)]) return; // SolidTile(x, y-1)
186	  if (st.inBounds(x, y + 1) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)]) return;   // !(x,y+1).active
187	  const preferSmall = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;
188	  const variation = rng.int(0, 2);
189	  const fx = variation * 18;
190	  st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, fx, 0);
191	  if (!preferSmall && st.inBounds(x, y + 1) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)]) {
192	    st.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, STALACTITE, fx, 18);
193	  }
194	}
195

1	// 全图液体沉降（从 Game.settleLiquids 平移的纯函数，主线程 fallback 与 worker 共用）。
2	// mode='gen'（新建世界）：原版 WorldGen pass 49/97 Settle Liquids（WorldGen.cs:16215+）——
3	//   外层 10 轮跑到收敛，每轮上限 活量×5，清除生成期晚到的回流（漏斗入口等）。
4	// mode='load'（读档）：原版 WorldFile.cs:738-770 时序 1:1——单轮上限 100000，
5	//   不收敛的少量残留活水交给运行时（尾部 WaterCheck），与原版一致（无外层循环）。
6	// yield 按真实耗时让出（≥32ms 一次）——worker 内也保留，让 progress postMessage 流出。
7	import type { World } from '../World';
8	import { LiquidSim } from './LiquidSim';
9	
10	export async function settleWorldLiquids(
11	  world: World,
12	  mode: 'gen' | 'load',
13	  onProgress?: (p: number) => void,
14	): Promise<void> {
15	  const sim = new LiquidSim(world);
16	  onProgress?.(0.05);
17	  sim.quickWater();
18	  onProgress?.(0.35);
19	  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
20	  sim.waterCheck();
21	  sim.quickSettle = true;
22	  // 轮上限：读档 = WorldFile 单轮 100000（L752）；生成期 = 每轮 活量×5（WorldGen.cs:16252 num5）
23	  const MAX_ROUNDS = mode === 'gen' ? 10 : 1; // 读档无外层循环（WorldFile.cs:746-762）
24	  let iter = 0;
25	  let lastYield = performance.now();
26	  for (let round = 0; round < MAX_ROUNDS; round++) {
27	    const MAX_ITER = mode === 'gen' ? Math.max(1000, (sim.numLiquid + sim.bufferLen) * 5) : 100000;
28	    let roundIter = 0;
29	    while (sim.numLiquid > 0 && roundIter < MAX_ITER) {
30	      sim.updateLiquid();
31	      iter++; roundIter++;
32	      if (roundIter % 2000 === 0) {
33	        onProgress?.(0.35 + Math.min(0.6, iter / 20000));
34	        if (performance.now() - lastYield >= 32) {
35	          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
36	          lastYield = performance.now();
37	        }
38	      }
39	    }
40	    sim.waterCheck();
41	    if (sim.numLiquid === 0) break; // 收敛即止
42	  }
43	  sim.quickSettle = false;
44	  sim.waterCheck(); // 残留活水交给运行时收敛（原版 WorldFile.cs:769 同款）
45	  sim.dispose();
46	  onProgress?.(1);
47	}
48

// 阶段2 验证:同 seed 二次生成逐 tile 一致(RNG 契约)+ 三种尺寸可生成
import puppeteer from 'puppeteer-core';
const browser = await puppeteer.launch({executablePath:'/Applications/Google Chrome.app/Contents/MacOS/Google Chrome', headless:'new', args:['--no-sandbox'], defaultViewport:{width:1280,height:800}});
const page = await browser.newPage();
const errs = [];
page.on('pageerror', e => errs.push(e.message));
await page.goto('http://localhost:5199', {waitUntil:'domcontentloaded', timeout:60000});
const out = await page.evaluate(async () => {
  const mod = await import('/src/world/gen/WorldGen.ts');
  const hash = (st) => {
    let h = 2166136261 >>> 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i += 7) {
      h = Math.imul(h ^ st.type[i], 16777619);
      h = Math.imul(h ^ st.liquid[i], 16777619);
    }
    return h >>> 0;
  };
  const results = {};
  // 确定性
  const a = await mod.generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'det' });
  const b = await mod.generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'det' });
  results.deterministic = hash(a.store) === hash(b.store);
  // 三尺寸
  for (const [w, h] of [[4200, 1200], [6400, 1800], [8400, 2400]]) {
    const t0 = Date.now();
    try {
      const wld = await mod.generateWorld({ width: w, height: h, seedText: 'size' });
      const st = wld.store;
      let bad = 0, water = 0;
      for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i += 11) { if (st.liquid[i]) water++; }
      results[`${w}x${h}`] = { ms: Date.now() - t0, spawn: [wld.spawnX, wld.spawnY], waterSample: water, err: null };
    } catch (e) {
      results[`${w}x${h}`] = { ms: Date.now() - t0, err: String(e) };
    }
  }
  // 经典回退开关
  try {
    const c = await mod.generateWorld({ width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'classic', lgcTerrain: false });
    results.classicFallback = { ok: c.store.type.length === 4200 * 1200 };
  } catch (e) { results.classicFallback = { err: String(e) }; }

/** 脚本化 rng：按序吐出预设值 */
function scriptedRng(values: number[]): RNG {
  let i = 0;
  return { next: () => values[i++ % values.length] } as unknown as RNG;
}

/** 计数经 h 引用读取（不能展开拷贝——拷贝后计数器不会更新） */
function makeHooks(): HiveBreakHooks & { h: { drops: number; bees: number[] } } {
  const h = { drops: 0, bees: [] as number[] };
  return {
    h,
    dropHiveItem: () => { h.drops++; },
    spawnBee: (_px: number, _py: number, r: RNG) => { h.bees.push(r.next() < 0.5 ? 210 : 211); },
  };
}

{

 "content": "// 临时探针：微光液体生成量 vs 沉降后存活量（验证后删除）\nimport { describe, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { settleWorldLiquids } from '../src/world/liquid/settle';\n\nfunction countShimmer(st: { liquid: Uint8Array; liquidType: Uint8Array }) {\n  let cells = 0, amt = 0, inSolid = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < st.liquid.length; i++) {\n    if (st.liquidType[i] === 4 && st.liquid[i] > 0) { cells++; amt += st.liquid[i]; }\n  }\n  return { cells, amt };\n}\n\ndescribe('shimmer probe', () => {\n  it('counts before/after settle', async () => {\n    for (const seed of ['probe-shimmer']) {\n      const snaps: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n      const t0 = Date.now();\n      const world = await generateWorld({\n        width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: seed,\n        onWorldPartial: (w, idx, name) => {\n          if (name === '微光') snaps['afterShimmerPass'] = countShimmer(w.store);\n          if (name === '清理') snaps['afterCleanup'] = countShimmer(w.store);\n          if (name === '水体沉降') snaps['afterMidSettle'] = countShimmer(w.store);\n        },\n      });\n      snaps['afterGenerate'] = countShimmer(world.store);\n      for (let r = 1; r <= 3; r++) {\n        await settleWorldLiquids(world, 'gen');\n        snaps[`afterSettle${r}`] = countShimmer(world.store);\n      }\n      snaps['ms'] = Date.now() - t0;\n      console.log(JSON.stringify({ seed, snaps }, null, 1));\n    }\n  }, 600000);\n});\n"
}

 "content": "// 临时探针：微光液体生成量 vs 沉降后存活量（验证后删除）\nimport { describe, it } from 'vitest';\nimport { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { LiquidSim } from '../src/world/liquid/LiquidSim';\nimport { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';\n\nfunction stat(st: any) {\n  let cells = 0, amt = 0, inSolid = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < st.liquid.length; i++) {\n    if (st.liquidType[i] === 4 && st.liquid[i] > 0) {\n      cells++; amt += st.liquid[i];\n      const t = st.type[i];\n      if (t !== 0 && TILE_DEFS[t]?.solid) inSolid++;\n    }\n  }\n  return { cells, amt, inSolid };\n}\n\ndescribe('shimmer probe', () => {\n  it('stage-by-stage from shimmer snapshot', async () => {\n    let liq: Uint8Array | null = null, lt: Uint8Array | null = null;\n    const world = await generateWorld({\n      width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: 'probe-shimmer',\n      onWorldPartial: (w, idx, name) => {\n        if (name === '微光') { liq = w.store.liquid.slice(); lt = w.store.liquidType.slice(); }\n      },\n    });\n    const st: any = world.store;\n    st.liquid.set(liq!); st.liquidType.set(lt!);\n    console.log('restored', JSON.stringify(stat(st)));\n    const sim = new LiquidSim(world);\n    sim.quickWater();\n    console.log('after quickWater', JSON.stringify(stat(st)));\n    sim.waterCheck();\n    console.log('after waterCheck', JSON.stringify(stat(st)), 'numLiquid', sim.numLiquid);\n    sim.quickSettle = true;\n    for (let r = 0; r < 200 && sim.numLiquid > 0; r++) sim.updateLiquid();\n    console.log('after updateLiquid xN', JSON.stringify(stat(st)), 'numLiquid', sim.numLiquid);\n    sim.waterCheck();\n    console.log('after waterCheck2', JSON.stringify(stat(st)), 'numLiquid', sim.numLiquid);\n    sim.quickSettle = false;\n    for (let r = 0; r < 500 && sim.numLiquid > 0; r++) sim.updateLiquid();\n    console.log('after runtime updates x500', JSON.stringify(stat(st)), 'numLiquid', sim.numLiquid);\n  }, 600000);\n});\n"
}

  return _cn;

function tileCounterNext(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (_cn >= TILE_COUNTER_MAX || x < 5 || x > st.w - 5 || y < 5 || y > st.h - 5) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  if (!st.flags[i] || !TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]]?.solid || !clearable(st, i)) return;
  for (let k = 0; k < _cn; k++) {
    if (_cx[k] === x && _cy[k] === y) return;
  }
  _cx[_cn] = x; _cy[_cn] = y; _cn++;
  tileCounterNext(st, x - 1, y);
  tileCounterNext(st, x + 1, y);
  tileCounterNext(st, x, y - 1);
  tileCounterNext(st, x, y + 1);
}

function tileCounterKill(st: TileStore): void {
  for (let k = 0; k < _cn; k++) {
    const i = st.idx(_cx[k], _cy[k]);
    st.flags[i] = 0;
    st.type[i] = 0;
  }
}

```

Confirmed root cause: `finalize()` zeroes ghost types (type!=0 with flags==0 → type=0) — that's why the final world has type=0 in the air cells and my restore experiment survived. But at the mid-settle pass (pass 9), ghost types still exist: the pool air cells have type=STONE, flags=0 → `waterCheck()` in LiquidSim sees TILE_DEFS[STONE].solid → zeroes ALL the shimmer liquid.

Note: our LiquidSim's solid checks use `st.type[i]` directly, but the store's canonical "air" predicate is flags-based (active bit). Vanilla `nactive()` = active && !inEdge. 	public static bool TryGrowingTreeByType(int treeTileType, int checkedX, int checkedY, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)
	{
		bool result = false;
		switch (treeTileType)
		{
		case 5:
			result = GrowTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 323:
			result = GrowPalmTree(checkedX, checkedY, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 587:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Ruby, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 588:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Diamond, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 583:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Topaz, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 584:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amethyst, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 589:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Amber, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 586:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Emerald, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 585:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.GemTree_Sapphire, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 596:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Sakura, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 616:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.VanityTree_Willow, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		case 634:
			result = GrowTreeWithSettings(checkedX, checkedY, GrowTreeSettings.Profiles.Tree_Ash, treeHeightAddon, ignoreWalls);
			break;
		}
		return result;
	}

	public static bool GrowTreeWithSettings(int checkedX, int checkedY, GrowTreeSettings settings, int treeHeightAddon = 0, bool ignoreWalls = false)
	{
		int num = checkedY;
		while (Main.tile[checkedX, checkedY].active() && Main.tile[checkedX, num].type == settings.SaplingTileType)
		{
			num++;
		}
		if (Main.tile[checkedX - 1, num - 1].liquid != 0 || Main.tile[checkedX, num - 1].liquid != 0 || Main.tile[checkedX + 1, num - 1].liquid != 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		Tile tile = Main.tile[checkedX, num];
		if (!tile.nactive() || tile.halfBrick() || tile.slope() != 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		bool flag = ignoreWalls || settings.WallTest(Main.tile[checkedX, num - 1].wall);
		if (!settings.GroundTest(tile.type) || !flag)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if ((!Main.tile[checkedX - 1, num].active() || !settings.GroundTest(Main.tile[checkedX - 1, num].type)) && (!Main.tile[checkedX + 1, num].active() || !settings.GroundTest(Main.tile[checkedX + 1, num].type)))
		{
			return false;
		}
		TileColorCache cache = Main.tile[checkedX, num].BlockColorAndCoating();
		if (Main.tenthAnniversaryWorld && !isGeneratingOrLoadingWorld && ((!Main.notTheBeesWorld && !Main.dontStarveWorld) || Main.remixWorld) && (settings.TreeTileType == 596 || settings.TreeTileType == 616))
		{
			cache.Color = (byte)genRand.Next(1, 13);
		}
		int num2 = 2;
		int num3 = genRand.Next(settings.TreeHeightMin, settings.TreeHeightMax + 1) + treeHeightAddon;
		if (SecretSeed.errorWorld.Enabled && genRand.Next(SecretSeed.Variations.errorWorldAdjustment(2.0)) == 0)
		{
			num3 = ((!((double)checkedY < Main.worldSurface)) ? genRand.Next(3, 15) : genRand.Next(3, 50));
		}
		int num4 = num3 + settings.TreeTopPaddingNeeded;
		if (!EmptyTileCheck(checkedX - num2, checkedX + num2, num - num4, num - 1, 20))
		{
			return false;
		}
		bool flag2 = false;
		bool flag3 = false;
		int num5;
		for (int i = num - num3; i < num; i++)
		{
			Tile tile2 = Main.tile[checkedX, i];
			tile2.active(active: true);
			tile2.type = settings.TreeTileType;
			tile2.UseBlockColors(cache);
			num5 = genRand.Next(3);
			int num6 = genRand.Next(10);
			if (i == num - 1 || i == num - num3)
			{
				num6 = 0;
			}
			while (((num6 == 5 || num6 == 7) && flag2) || ((num6 == 6 || num6 == 7) && flag3))
			{
				num6 = genRand.Next(10);
			}
			flag2 = false;
			flag3 = false;
			if (num6 == 5 || num6 == 7)
			{
				flag2 = true;
			}
			if (num6 == 6 || num6 == 7)
			{
				flag3 = true;
			}
			switch (num6)
			{
			case 1:
				if (num5 == 0)
				{
					tile2.frameX = 0;
					tile2.frameY = 66;
				}
				if (num5 == 1)
				{
					tile2.frameX = 0;
					tile2.frameY = 88;
				}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T07:45:02.133Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
The GrowTreeWithSettings algorithm is fully self-contained and frame-based (frameX/frameY on 22px grid for a 3-frame-wide texture sheet). Gem trees are single-tile framed objects: trunk column with side branches, top cap. Tile 583-589 defs in our data have `framed: true, w:1, h:1, vanilla: {sheet, frame: 'style'}`.

Interesting: gem tree tiles are placed as individual 1x1 tiles with frameX/frameY from a 22px grid — so the whole tree is per-tile sprites. That means our ShimmerPass can place them with setTileSilent(x, y, tileId, fx, fy) using the same frame coordinates!

This is actually quite portable 1:1 (modulo EmptyTileCheck and IsTileTypeFitForTree and wall test). Let me check:
- GemTreeWallTest
- IsTileTypeFitForTree
- EmptyTileCheck

Then check our TreePass for how it places tree tiles (does our store support frameX/frameY for these gem trees? setTileSilent(x, y, type, fx, fy)).

Also — do we even render gem tree tiles? That's the renderer's concern; our tiles data has them defined with frame 'style'. Rendering may or may not be wired, but that's out of scope; the pass-level placement is the task.

Let me read GemTreeWallTest, IsTileTypeFitForTree, EmptyTileCheck.
```

</details>

			WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[i];
			if (EligibleForSpawnProtection(worldItem))
			{
				worldItem.timeSinceItemSpawned -= 18000;
			}
		}
	}

	private static bool EligibleForSpawnProtection(WorldItem item)
	{
		if (item.active)
		{
			return ItemID.Sets.OverflowProtectionTimeOffset[item.type] == 0;
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static void UndoSpawnedItemProtection()
	{
		for (int i = 0; i < 400; i++)
		{
			WorldItem worldItem = Main.item[i];
			if (EligibleForSpawnProtection(worldItem) && worldItem.timeSinceItemSpawned < 0)
			{
				worldItem.timeSinceItemSpawned += 18000;
			}

// RNG 映射：Next(a,b) → rng.int(a, b-1)。
import { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
import type { GenState } from './GenState';

import { T, TILE_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/tiles';

const TREE = TILE_BY_KEY['tree']!;              // tile 5

/** IsTileTypeFitForTree（L14147）：草族 9 种 */

/** GrowTree 头部墙白名单（L14176：0/80/106/107/138-141/145/150/152） */
const TREE_WALLS = new Set([0, 80, 106, 107, 138, 139, 140, 141, 145, 150, 152]);
/** 棕榈基座沙族（L11793：53/234/116/112） */
const PALM_GROUND = idsByVanillaSheet(53, 234, 116, 112);
const JUNGLE_GRASS = idsByVanillaSheet(60);
/** 植物（vanilla tile 20）：GrowTree 头部越过找基座（原版 while type==20） */
const PLANTS = idsByVanillaSheet(20);

/** 干身帧表：num5 case → num4(变体 0-2) → [fx,fy]（L14213-14425 逐 case 提取） */
const TRUNK: Record<number, Array<[number, number]>> = {
  0: [[0, 0], [0, 22], [0, 44]],
  1: [[0, 66], [0, 88], [0, 110]],
  2: [[22, 0], [22, 22], [22, 44]],
  3: [[44, 66], [44, 88], [44, 110]],
  4: [[22, 66], [22, 88], [22, 110]],
  5: [[88, 0], [88, 22], [88, 44]],
  6: [[66, 66], [66, 88], [66, 110]],
  7: [[110, 66], [110, 88], [110, 110]],
  8: [[0, 0], [0, 22], [0, 44]],
  9: [[0, 0], [0, 22], [0, 44]],
};

/** EmptyTileCheck（L15685，ignoreID=-1 简化：出界 false，任何实心 false） */
function emptyTileCheck(st: TileStore, x0: number, x1: number, y0: number, y1: number): boolean {
  if (x0 < 0 || x1 >= st.w || y0 < 0 || y1 >= st.h) return false;
  for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
      if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) return false;
    }
  }
  return true;
}

/** 树干+枝+根+冠 共体（GrowTree L14203-14606 / GrowEpicTree 同表）。
 *  groundType：基座 tile（判丛林加高）；返回是否成树 */
function growTrunk(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, index1: number, height: number): boolean {
  let flagL = false, flagR = false; // 侧枝去重（num5 5/7 左、6/7 右不同帧连续限制）
  for (let y = index1 - height; y < index1; y++) {
    // num4 = 枝型 0-9；顶/底强制 0；5/7 与 6/7 连续出现需重投
    let num5 = rng.int(0, 9);
    if (y === index1 - 1 || y === index1 - height) num5 = 0;
    while (((num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) && flagL) || ((num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) && flagR)) {
      num5 = rng.int(0, 9);
    }
    flagL = false; flagR = false;
    if (num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) flagL = true;
    if (num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) flagR = true;
    const num4 = rng.int(0, 2);
    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num5][num4];
    st.setTileSilent(i, y, TREE, fx, fy);
    // 侧枝帧（L14362-14426）：Next(3)<2 走 198+ 行（贴干枝），否则行内变体
    if (num5 === 5 || num5 === 7) {
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [44, 198 + k * 22] : [66, k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(i - 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);
    }
    if (num5 === 6 || num5 === 7) {
      const k = rng.int(0, 2);
      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ? [66, 198 + k * 22] : [88, 66 + k * 22];
      st.setTileSilent(i + 1, y, TREE, bfx, bfy);
    }
  }
  // 基座方向 num7（L14439-14543）：两侧地面适配 → 决定根部与基座帧。
  //  侧根放置前提 = 邻格整砖(L30230/30234:!halfBrick && slope==0)——半砖/坡面侧不出根
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;
    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);
    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);
  };
  let num7 = rng.int(0, 2);
  const sideL = fitSide(-1), sideR = fitSide(1);
  if (!sideL) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 2; else if (num7 === 1) num7 = 3; }
  if (!sideR) { if (num7 === 0) num7 = 1; else if (num7 === 2) num7 = 3; }
  if (sideL && !sideR) num7 = 2;
  if (sideR && !sideL) num7 = 1;
  const k8 = rng.int(0, 2);
  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 1) st.setTileSilent(i + 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 22, 132 + k8 * 22); // 右根
  if (num7 === 0 || num7 === 2) st.setTileSilent(i - 1, index1 - 1, TREE, 44, 132 + k8 * 22); // 左根
  // 基座干身（switch num7，L14519-14566）
  const baseFx = num7 === 0 ? 88 : num7 === 1 ? 0 : num7 === 2 ? 66 : -1;
  if (baseFx >= 0) st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, TREE, baseFx, 132 + k8 * 22);
  // 树冠标记帧（L14568-14600）：12/13 fx=22，否则 fx=0；fy=198+22*k
  const crownFx = rng.int(0, 12) !== 0 ? 22 : 0;
  st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - height, TREE, crownFx, 198 + rng.int(0, 2) * 22);
  gs.genTrees.push({ x: i, y: index1 - height, h: height }); // 砍伐整棵判定用
  return true;
}

/** GrowTree（L14171）：常规树。height=Next(5,17)；丛林草基座净空 +5 */
function growTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, y: number): boolean {
  let index1 = y;
  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;

  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !FIT.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  if (!TREE_WALLS.has(st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)])) return false;
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => {
    if (!st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)) return false;
    const si = st.idx(i + dx, index1);
    return !!st.flags[si] && !st.half[si] && st.slope[si] === 0 && FIT.has(st.type[si]);
  };
  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;

  } catch (err) {

      // 背景 pass 不透明（露出方块透明像素 = 浸润）；前景 pass 水 0.6 半透明质感
      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, opacity[i] * (isBackground ? 1 : (DEFAULT_OPACITY[vt] ?? 0.6)));
      ctx.drawImage(tex, sx, srcY, sw, sh,
        x * 16 + Math.floor(n2 * 16), y * 16 + Math.floor(n4 * 16), sw, sh);
    }
  }

  // ---- 浸润 pass：DrawTile_LiquidBehindTile(TileDrawing.cs:3859-4193) ----
  // 对每个【实心方块格】，检查四邻是否有液体 → 在方块格内画一条液体带
  // （背景 pass，不透明）。方块贴图画在其上——透明圆角像素透出液体色而不是墙色/背景色。
  // 这是原版"水体包裹方块"的全部秘密；条带宽度由邻接方向决定：
  //   仅上方有水 → 格顶 16×4 横条(:4081-4087)
  //   仅下方有水 → 格底 16×4 横条(:4089-4093)
  //   仅左侧有水 → 格左 4px 竖条(:4113-4116);仅右侧 → 格右 4px(:4118-4121)
  //   左右都有   → 整格 16 宽(:4108-4111);深度按液体量 num6=(256-max)/32*2 从底收
  if (isBackground) drawLiquidBehindTiles(ctx, atlas, st, groundLevel, tx0, ty0, tx1, ty1);

  ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
}

/**
 * 浸润 pass:原版 TilesRenderer.DrawLiquidBehindTiles → DrawTile_LiquidBehindTile
 * (TileDrawing.cs:3859-4193)。对视区内每个实心方块格检查四邻液体,在方块格内画液体带。
 * 背景 pass 调用(画在方块贴图之下)——透明圆角像素透出液体色。
 */
function drawLiquidBehindTiles(
  ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas,
  st: TileStore, groundLevel: number,
  tx0: number, ty0: number, tx1: number, ty1: number,
): void {
  // Liquid_N 贴图(16×16 纯液体块;水 Liquid_0 多 style 但取首 16px)
  const texCache = new Map<number, HTMLImageElement | null>();
  const texFor = (vt: number) => {
    let t = texCache.get(vt);
    if (t === undefined) {
      const file = vt === 1 ? 'vanilla/Liquid_1.png' : vt === 2 ? 'vanilla/Liquid_11.png' : 'vanilla/Liquid_0.png';
      t = atlas.ensureVImage(file) ?? null;
      if (t) texCache.set(vt, t); // 只缓存命中(缓存 null 会把未就绪永久化——首帧丢条带)
    }
    return t;
  };

  for (let x = Math.max(1, tx0); x <= Math.min(st.w - 2, tx1); x++) {
    for (let y = Math.max(1, ty0); y <= Math.min(st.h - 2, ty1); y++) {
      const i = st.idx(x, y);
      if (!st.flags[i]) continue; // 空格没有"方块后面"
      const def = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
      if (!def || !def.solid || def.platform) continue; // 仅实心方块(:4075 tileSolid 排除)

      // 四邻液体(TileDrawing.cs:3861-3900;379=conveyor 按 vanilla 视为无液体)
      const lq = (dx: number, dy: number) => {
        const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
        if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) return { lq: 0, lt: 0 };
        const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);
        return { lq: st.liquid[ni], lt: st.liquidType[ni] || 1 };
      };
      const L = lq(-1, 0), R = lq(1, 0), U = lq(0, -1), D = lq(0, 1);
      // flag 语义(:3967-4053):flag=左 / flag2=右 / flag3=上 / flag4=下(>240)
      let fL = L.lq > 0; let fR = R.lq > 0; const fU = U.lq > 0, fD = D.lq > 240;
      if (!fL && !fR && !fU && !fD) continue; // :4055-4057 四邻无液体跳过

      // 液体类型与最大液位(:3940-3965 取最高液位邻居)
      let maxLq = 0, lt = 0;
      const consider = (n: { lq: number; lt: number }) => {
        if (n.lq > maxLq) { maxLq = n.lq; lt = toVanillaType(n.lt); }
      };
      if (fL) consider(L); if (fR) consider(R); if (fU) consider(U); if (fD) consider(D);
      if (lt === 3) continue; // 微光暂不支持
      const tex = texFor(lt);
      if (!tex) continue;

      // ---- 液体矩形计算(:4069-4123) ----
      // 原版默认 rect = (0, 4, 16, 16);pos = (tileX*16, tileY*16)
      let rx = 0, ry = 4, rw = 16, rh = 16;   // liquidSize(源矩形)
      let px = x * 16, py = y * 16;            // 目标位置

      if (fD && (fL || fR)) { fL = true; fR = true; /* :4070-4074 下+任一侧 → 双侧全宽 */ }
      if (!(fU && (fL || fR)) && !(fD && fU)) {
        if (fU) {
          // 仅上方有水:格顶 16×4 条(:4081-4087;坡面/半砖加深到 12)
          rh = 4;
          const isHalf = st.half[i];
          if (isHalf) rh = 12;
        } else if (fD && !fL && !fR) {
          // 仅下方:格底 16×4(:4089-4093)
          py = y * 16 + 12; rh = 4;
        } else {
          // 侧向有水(:4095-4123)
          let y0 = 4;
          const upNi = st.idx(x, y - 1);
          if (st.liquid[upNi] === 0 && !st.isSolid(x, y - 1)) y0 = 0; // 上方无液无实心→从格顶画(:4098-4101)
          const num6 = Math.floor((256 - maxLq) / 32) * 2; // 深度=液位不足时从底收(:4096,4102)

```

		{
			SpecialLiquidDrawCache* ptr2 = ptr;

				}
				Main.tileBatch.SetLayer(0u, 0);

					sourceRectangle.Y = 1280;

	public static VertexColors SetShimmerVertexColors_Sparkle(ref VertexColors colors, float opacity, int x, int y, bool top)
	{
		colors.BottomLeftColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x, y + 1);
		colors.BottomRightColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x + 1, y + 1);
		colors.TopLeftColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x, y);
		colors.TopRightColor = GetShimmerGlitterColor(top, x + 1, y);
		colors.BottomLeftColor *= opacity;
		colors.BottomRightColor *= opacity;
		colors.TopLeftColor *= opacity;
		colors.TopRightColor *= opacity;
		return colors;
	}

	public static void SetShimmerVertexColors(ref VertexColors colors, float opacity, int x, int y)
	{
		colors.BottomLeftColor = Color.White;
		colors.BottomRightColor = Color.White;
		colors.TopLeftColor = Color.White;
		colors.TopRightColor = Color.White;
		colors.BottomLeftColor *= opacity;
		colors.BottomRightColor *= opacity;
		colors.TopLeftColor *= opacity;
		colors.TopRightColor *= opacity;
		colors.BottomLeftColor = new Color(colors.BottomLeftColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x, y + 1));
		colors.BottomRightColor = new Color(colors.BottomRightColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x + 1, y + 1));
		colors.TopLeftColor = new Color(colors.TopLeftColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x, y));
		colors.TopRightColor = new Color(colors.TopRightColor.ToVector4() * GetShimmerBaseColor(x + 1, y));
	}

	public static float GetShimmerWave(ref float worldPositionX, ref float worldPositionY)
	{
		return (float)Math.Sin(((double)((worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) / 10f) - Main.timeForVisualEffects / 360.0) * 6.2831854820251465);
	}

	public static Color GetShimmerGlitterColor(bool top, float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
	{
		Color color = Main.hslToRgb((float)(((double)(worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) + Main.timeForVisualEffects / 30.0) / 6.0) % 1f, 1f, 0.5f);
		color.A = 0;
		return new Color(color.ToVector4() * GetShimmerGlitterOpacity(top, worldPositionX, worldPositionY));
	}

	public static float GetShimmerGlitterOpacity(bool top, float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
	{
		if (top)
		{
			return 0.5f;
		}
		float num = Utils.Remap((float)Math.Sin(((double)((worldPositionX + worldPositionY / 6f) / 10f) - Main.timeForVisualEffects / 360.0) * 6.2831854820251465), -0.5f, 1f, 0f, 0.35f);

	}

	private static uint SimpleWhiteNoise(uint x, uint y)
	{
		x = 36469 * (x & 0xFFFF) + (x >> 16);
		y = 18012 * (y & 0xFFFF) + (y >> 16);
		return (x << 16) + y;
	}

	public int GetShimmerFrame(bool top, float worldPositionX, float worldPositionY)
	{

		}

	}

    const y0 = Math.max(2, ty0 - WATERFALL_DIST), y1 = Math.min(st.h - 3, ty1 + 20);
    const solid = (x: number, y: number) => {
      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
      const t = st.type[st.idx(x, y)];
      if (t === 0) return false;
      const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
      return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform;
    };
    for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {
      for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {
        const i = st.idx(x, y);
        if (!st.flags[i] || !st.half[i]) continue;          // active && halfBrick（L102）
        const ui = i - st.w;
        const uq = y > 0 ? st.liquid[ui] : 0;
        if (!(uq < 16 || solid(x, y - 1))) continue;          // 上方近干或实心（L110）
        const li = i - 1, ri = i + 1;
        const lq = st.liquid[li], rq = st.liquid[ri];
        const lOpen = lq === 0 && !solid(x - 1, y) && st.slope[li] === 0;
        const rOpen = rq === 0 && !solid(x + 1, y) && st.slope[ri] === 0;
        if (!((lq > 160 || rq > 160) && (lOpen || rOpen))) continue; // L124
        // 类型：上/右/左三格任一岩浆→1 蜂蜜→14，否则水（L126-127）
        let type = 0;
        const isLava = (ii: number) => st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 2;
        const isHoney = (ii: number) => st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === 3;
        if (isLava(ui) || isLava(ri) || isLava(li)) type = 1;
        else if (isHoney(ui) || isHoney(ri) || isHoney(li)) type = 2;
        this.falls.push({ x, y, type });
        if (this.falls.length >= MAX_FALLS) return;
      }
    }
  }

  /** 绘制（DrawWaterfall 行 211-803 的主体路径；style 固定 0=水） */
  draw(ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, atlas: SpriteAtlas | null, st: TileStore, groundLevel: number, nowMs: number) {
    if (!atlas || this.falls.length === 0) return;
    const tex = SHEET.map((s) => atlas.ensureVImage(s) ?? null);
    if (!tex[0] && !tex[1] && !tex[2]) return;
    // 帧动画：水 3 tick/帧、岩浆蜂蜜 7 tick/帧（1 tick ≈ 16.67ms）
    const regular = Math.floor(nowMs / 50) % 16;
    const slow = Math.floor(nowMs / 117) % 16;
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;

      const texImg = tex[wf.type] ?? tex[0];
      if (!texImg) continue;

// 逐 pass 网格哈希检查点（种子等价 L1 工具）：
// generateWorld 的 onWorldPartial 在每个 pass 后回调——对 type/flags/wall/liquid
// 四数组做 FNV-1a，输出「pass 名 → 哈希」清单。C# oracle 逐段延伸后，
// 本清单与 oracle 各段哈希逐项比对即可二分定位首个分歧 pass。
// 跑法：npx vitest run tests/pass-hash.test.ts（控制台输出哈希表）
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { generateWorld } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';

export function fnv1a(arr: ArrayLike<number>, mod = 2 ** 32): string {
  let h = 0x811c9dc5 >>> 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
    h ^= arr[i] & 0xff;
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
    h ^= (arr[i] >>> 8) & 0xff;
    h = Math.imul(h, 0x01000193) >>> 0;
    // Uint16Array 上 16 位全吃；Uint8Array 高 8 位自然为 0
  }
  return h.toString(16).padStart(8, '0');
}

describe('逐 pass 网格哈希检查点', () => {
  it('种子 9293480：每 pass 后 type/flags/wall/liquid FNV 哈希', async () => {
    const checkpoints: Array<{ pass: string; hash: string }> = [];
    const world = await generateWorld({
      width: 4200, height: 1200, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0,
      onWorldPartial: (w, _idx, name) => {
        const st = w.store;
        checkpoints.push({
          pass: name,
          hash: [fnv1a(st.type), fnv1a(st.flags), fnv1a(st.wall), fnv1a(st.liquid)].join(' '),
        });
      },
    });
    const st = world.store;
    checkpoints.push({ pass: 'finalize', hash: [fnv1a(st.type), fnv1a(st.flags), fnv1a(st.wall), fnv1a(st.liquid)].join(' ') });
    for (const c of checkpoints) console.log(`${c.pass.padEnd(6, '　')} ${c.hash}`);
    expect(checkpoints.length).toBeGreaterThan(10);
  }, 600000);
});

```

// 世界门面：TileStore + 元数据 + 实体容器 + 昼夜时钟
import { TileStore } from './TileStore';
import { WeatherState, applyWeatherSave, type WeatherSave } from './weather/Weather';
import { T, TILE_DEFS } from '../data/tiles';

export interface ChestData { x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>; locked?: boolean; }

export class Clock {
  timeOfDay: number = 0.3;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始上午 7:12 ≈ 0.3）
  dayCount: number = 1;
  /** 月相 0-7（0=满月 4=新月；每黎明 +1 mod 8，Main.cs:64877）。对话月相池/血月门用 */
  moonPhase: number = 0;
  /** 血月旗标（dusk roll / dawn 清除，Main.cs:64817-64831） */
  bloodMoon: boolean = false;
  /** 圣诞季（现实日期 12/15-31，checkXMas Main.cs:13054） */
  xMas: boolean = false;
  /** 万圣季（现实日期 10/20-11/10，checkHalloween Main.cs:13093） */
  halloween: boolean = false;
  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
  tick(dtMs: number) {
    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
  }
  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > 0.25 && this.timeOfDay < 0.75; }
  get dayFactor(): number {
    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
    const t = this.timeOfDay;
    if (t > 0.28 && t < 0.72) return 1;
    if (t >= 0.72 && t < 0.80) return 1 - (t - 0.72) / 0.08 * 0.68;
    if (t >= 0.80 || t < 0.20) return 0.32;
    return 0.32 + (t - 0.20) / 0.08 * 0.68;
  }
  get hourFloat(): number { return this.timeOfDay * 24; }
}

export class World {
  store: TileStore;
  name: string;
  seed: number;
  spawnX = 0; spawnY = 0;
  groundLevel = 0; rockLevel = 0;   // tile 坐标
  /** 地狱顶（原版 UnderworldLayer = maxTilesY-200；TerrainPass 设定，SceneMetrics/BGM/背景共用） */
  lavaLine = 0;
  clock: Clock;
  /** 天气系统（WeatherState 状态机；存档/wld 导入经 applyWeatherSave 回填） */
  weather = new WeatherState();
  applyWeather(s: Partial<WeatherSave>) { applyWeatherSave(this.weather, s); }
  chests: ChestData[] = [];
  /** 墓碑碑文（原版 Sign 系统的最小子集）：锚点 = 墓碑 tile 左上格 */
  signs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; text: string }> = [];
  /** 读档/wld 导入携带的城镇 NPC（afterWorldLoad 原位生成后清空；原版 WorldFile town NPC 段） */
  pendingTownNpcs?: Array<{ key: string; x: number; y: number; home: [number, number] | null; bound?: boolean; givenName?: string }>;
  // Boss 进度旗标
  flags: Record<string, boolean> = { downedEyeOfCthulhu: false, downedSkeletron: false, shadowOrbSmashed: false, hardMode: false };
  // 树登记：砍树干时找到整棵树（roots → 范围）
  trees: Array<{ x: number; y: number; h: number }> = [];
  /** 战争迷雾：1 = 已探索。按 tile 粒度。 */
  explored: Uint8Array;
  /** 原版树样式数据（header treeX/treeStyle）：横向 4 区森林树冠样式 */
src/workers/worldPacket.ts
src/workers/worldGen.worker.ts

private shimmerCheck(x, y) {

  const lq, rq, uq, dq; lt, rt, ut, dt;
  if ((lq>0 && lt!==4) || (rq>0 && rt!==4) || (uq>0 && ut!==4)) {
    let sum = 0;

    if (ut!==4) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu]=0; }

}

 "new_string": "  // ================= 液体交互（Liquid.cs:888-1115 + GetLiquidMergeTypes 1385-1453） =================\n  // 合并产物优先级（GetLiquidMergeTypes 逐段覆盖，后写胜出）：\n  //   水 > 岩浆 > 蜂蜜 > 微光——任一方是微光 → Aetherium 块 659；\n  //   否则 蜂蜜在场 → 229/230，只剩水岩组合 → 黑曜石 56\n\n  /** 岩浆格与水/蜂蜜/微光接触 → 黑曜石 56 / 脆蜂蜜块 230 / Aetherium 659（阈值 24） */\n  private lavaCheck(x: number, y: number) {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:898）\n    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);\n    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;\n    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];\n    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];\n    if ((lq > 0 && lt !== 2) || (rq > 0 && rt !== 2) || (uq > 0 && ut !== 2)) {\n      // 情形 A：左右/上方有非岩浆液体 → 生成在岩浆格（Liquid.cs:905-959）\n      let sum = 0;\n      let type = OBSIDIAN;\n      if (lt !== 2) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }\n      if (rt !== 2) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }\n      if (ut !== 2) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }\n      if (lt === 3 || rt === 3 || ut === 3) type = CRISPY_HONEY;\n      if (lt === 4 || rt === 4 || ut === 4) type = AETHERIUM; // 微光优先级最高（L1443-1451）\n      if (sum < 24) return;\n      const t = st.type[i5];\n      if (t !== 0) {\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];\n        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);  // 近似 tileObsidianKill\n        else return;                             // 平台等保留方块 → 不生成\n      }\n      st.liquid[i5] = 0;\n      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;\n      st.setTile(x, y, type);\n    } else {\n      if (dq <= 0 || dt === 2) return;\n      // 情形 B：仅下方有水/蜂蜜/微光 → 生成在下方格（Liquid.cs:961-1014）\n      const belowT = st.type[id];\n      if (belowT !== 0) {\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];\n        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0); // tileCut / obsidianKill 近似\n        else return;\n      }\n      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }\n      const type = dt === 4 ? AETHERIUM : dt === 3 ? CRISPY_HONEY : OBSIDIAN;\n      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;\n      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;\n      st.setTile(x, y + 1, type);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 蜂蜜格与水/岩浆/微光接触 → 蜂蜜块 229 / 脆蜂蜜块 230 / Aetherium 659（阈值 24）。\n   *  触发门 = 任一邻格液体类型非蜂蜜（LiquidCheck L1244 `liquidType() != thisLiquidType`），\n   *  此前只识别水邻——岩浆/微光邻漏触发，1456 对齐补齐 */\n  private honeyCheck(x: number, y: number) {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return; // WorldGen.SolidTile：半砖/坡面放行（Liquid.cs:1020）\n    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);\n    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;\n    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];\n    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];\n    if ((lq > 0 && lt !== 3) || (rq > 0 && rt !== 3) || (uq > 0 && ut !== 3)) {\n      let sum = 0;\n      if (lt !== 3) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }\n      if (rt !== 3) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }\n      if (ut !== 3) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }\n      if (sum < 24) return; // 1456 LiquidCheck 统一阈值 24\n      const type = (lt === 4 || rt === 4 || ut === 4) ? AETHERIUM\n        : (lt === 2 || rt === 2 || ut === 2) ? CRISPY_HONEY : HONEY_BLOCK;\n      const t = st.type[i5];\n      if (t !== 0) {\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];\n        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);\n        else return;\n      }\n      st.liquid[i5] = 0;\n      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;\n      st.setTile(x, y, type);\n    } else {\n      if (dq <= 0 || dt === 3) return;\n      const belowT = st.type[id];\n      if (belowT !== 0) {\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];\n        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0);\n        else return;\n      }\n      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; } // 1456 统一 24\n      const type = dt === 4 ? AETHERIUM : dt === 2 ? CRISPY_HONEY : HONEY_BLOCK;\n      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;\n      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;\n      st.setTile(x, y + 1, type);\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 微光格与水/岩浆/蜂蜜任一接触 → Aetherium 块 659（Liquid.cs:1475-1478 →\n   *  LiquidCheck(thisLiquidType=3)；GetLiquidMergeTypes L1437-1452 微光行恒为 659） */\n  private shimmerCheck(x: number, y: number) {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    if (this.solidTileFull(x, y)) return;\n    const i5 = this.idx(x, y);\n    const il = i5 - 1, ir = i5 + 1, iu = i5 - st.w, id = i5 + st.w;\n    const lq = st.liquid[il], rq = st.liquid[ir], uq = st.liquid[iu], dq = st.liquid[id];\n    const lt = st.liquidType[il], rt = st.liquidType[ir], ut = st.liquidType[iu], dt = st.liquidType[id];\n    if ((lq > 0 && lt !== 4) || (rq > 0 && rt !== 4) || (uq > 0 && ut !== 4)) {\n      // 情形 A：左右/上方有非微光液体 → 汇总清零，产物生成在微光格（Liquid.cs:1250-1273）\n      let sum = 0;\n      if (lt !== 4) { sum += lq; st.liquid[il] = 0; }\n      if (rt !== 4) { sum += rq; st.liquid[ir] = 0; }\n      if (ut !== 4) { sum += uq; st.liquid[iu] = 0; }\n      // mergeType == thisType 门（L1269）：三邻中仍有微光 → 不产块（liquidMergeType 被拉回 3）\n      if (sum < 24 || lt === 4 || rt === 4 || ut === 4) return;\n      const t = st.type[i5];\n      if (t !== 0) {\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[t];\n        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y, 0);  // 近似 tileObsidianKill\n        else return;\n      }\n      st.liquid[i5] = 0;\n      st.liquidType[i5] = 0;\n      st.setTile(x, y, AETHERIUM);\n    } else {\n      if (dq <= 0 || dt === 4) return;\n      // 情形 B：仅下方有异种液体 → 产物生成在下方格（Liquid.cs:1275-1317）\n      const belowT = st.type[id];\n      if (belowT !== 0) {\n        const d = TILE_DEFS[belowT];\n        if (d && d.decor) st.setTile(x, y + 1, 0); // tileCut / obsidianKill 近似\n        else return;\n      }\n      if (st.liquid[i5] < 24) { st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0; return; }\n      st.liquid[i5] = 0; st.liquidType[i5] = 0;\n      st.liquid[id] = 0; st.liquidType[id] = 0;\n      st.setTile(x, y + 1, AETHERIUM);\n    }\n  }"
}

{
 "replace_all": false,
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/liquid/LiquidSim.ts",
 "old_string": "  /** 自底向上逐湿格直接搬运沉降（原版 QuickWater(verbose, -1, -1)：y 从 h-3 到 3） */\n  quickWater(minY = 3, maxY = -1) {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const yMax = maxY < 0 ? st.h - 3 : maxY;\n    for (let y = yMax; y >= minY; y--) {\n      for (let x = 4; x < st.w - 4; x++) {\n        if (st.liquid[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0) this.settleWaterAt(x, y);\n      }\n    }\n  }",
 "new_string": "  /** 自底向上逐湿格直接搬运沉降（原版 QuickWater(verbose, -1, -1)：y 从 h-3 到 3）。\n   *  生成期首尾各调一次 ShimmerRemoveWater（Liquid.cs:107/137 + WorldGen.cs:34859） */\n  quickWater(minY = 3, maxY = -1) {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    if (this.generatingWorld) this.shimmerRemoveWater();\n    const yMax = maxY < 0 ? st.h - 3 : maxY;\n    for (let y = yMax; y >= minY; y--) {\n      for (let x = 4; x < st.w - 4; x++) {\n        if (st.liquid[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0) this.settleWaterAt(x, y);\n      }\n    }\n    if (this.generatingWorld) this.shimmerRemoveWater();\n  }\n\n  /** WorldGen.ShimmerRemoveWater（cs:34859-34886）：以太腔 150 半径球内清除\n   *  非微光液体 + 去活薄冰 162——防沉降期周边湖/洞穴水灌入腔体与微光互转 Aetherium */\n  private shimmerRemoveWater() {\n    const c = this.shimmerCenter;\n    if (!c) return;\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const R = 150;\n    const x0 = c.x - R, x1 = c.x + R, y0 = c.y - R, y1 = c.y + R / 2;\n    for (let y = y0; y <= y1; y++) {\n      for (let x = x0; x <= x1; x++) {\n        if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;\n        // 原版 (int)Math.Sqrt(dx²+dy²) < 150（整数平方根比较）\n        const dx = Math.abs(x - c.x), dy = Math.abs(y - c.y);\n        if (Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)) >= R) continue;\n        const i = st.idx(x, y);\n        if (st.liquidType[i] !== 4) st.liquid[i] = 0;\n        if (st.type[i] === THIN_ICE) st.flags[i] = 0; // active(false)——幽灵 type 由 finalize 归零\n      }\n    }\n  }"
}
