/** GrowEpicTree（L11850）：巨型树。基座严格草(2)、邻格草族、净空 55、height=Next(20,30) */
function growEpicTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, i: number, y: number): boolean {
  let index1 = y;
  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
  const GRASS = idsByVanillaSheet(2);
  // 基座整砖检查(GrowEpicTree 同 GrowTree 头部语义:半砖/坡面拒绝)
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !GRASS.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
  const family = idsByVanillaSheet(2, 23, 60, 109);
  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(i + dx, index1)
    && st.flags[st.idx(i + dx, index1)] && family.has(st.type[st.idx(i + dx, index1)]);
  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 2, i + 2, index1 - 55, index1 - 1)) return false;
  return growTrunk(st, rng, gs, i, index1, rng.int(20, 29)); // Next(20,30)
}

/** GrowPalmTree（L11785）：沙滩棕榈，干身倾斜帧 fy=num4（±2 步进趋向 num3） */
function growPalmTree(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, i: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(i, y)) return false;
  let index1 = y;
  while (st.inBounds(i, index1) && st.flags[st.idx(i, index1)] && PLANTS.has(st.type[st.idx(i, index1)])) index1++;
  if (!st.inBounds(i, index1) || index1 < 1) return false;
  const gi = st.idx(i, index1);
  // 基座整砖检查(GrowPalmTree:27280-27285 active && !halfBrick && slope==0)
  if (!st.flags[gi] || st.half[gi] || st.slope[gi] !== 0 || !PALM_GROUND.has(st.type[gi])) return false;
  if (st.wall[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] !== 0 || st.liquid[st.idx(i, index1 - 1)] > 0) return false;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i, i, index1 - 2, index1 - 1)) return false;
  if (!emptyTileCheck(st, i - 1, i + 1, index1 - 30, index1 - 3)) return false;
  const height = rng.int(10, 20);                 // Next(10,21)
  const target = rng.int(-8, 8) * 2;              // Next(-8,9)*2
  let tilt = 0;
  for (let k = 0; k < height; k++) {
    if (k === 0) {
      st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1, PALM, 66, 0);
      continue;
    }
    if (k === height - 1) {
      st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(4, 6), tilt); // 顶段 fx=22*Next(4,7)
      continue;
    }
    if (tilt !== target) {
      const prog = k / height;                    // 倾斜步进阈值（L11814-11820）
      if (prog >= 0.25 && ((prog < 0.5 && rng.int(0, 12) === 0)
        || (prog < 0.7 && rng.int(0, 8) === 0)
        || (prog < 0.95 && rng.int(0, 4) === 0) || true)) {
        tilt += Math.sign(target) * 2;
      }
    }
    st.setTileSilent(i, index1 - 1 - k, PALM, 22 * rng.int(0, 2), tilt); // fx=22*Next(0,3)
  }
  return true;
}

/** AddTrees（L15642）：逐列尝试（~1.6 列间距）；沙滩棕榈按每侧固定概率；
 *  草上 1/20 观赏树（柳/樱 GrowTreeWithSettings 未移植——缺对应生成管线，留待 C 批） */
export function runTreePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  // EpicTree 林区（pass 82 头段，L9656-9666）：w*0.003 个 25-50 半宽林区逐格尝试
  for (let n = 0; n < st.w * 0.003; n++) {
    const cx = rng.int(50, st.w - 51);            // Next(50, maxTilesX-50)
    const hw = rng.int(25, 49);                  // Next(25, 50)
    for (let x = cx - hw; x < cx + hw; x++) {
      for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) growEpicTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
    }
  }
  const palmL = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;          // 每侧棕榈概率（L15644-45）
  const palmR = 0.1 + rng.next() * 0.35;
  for (let x = 1; x < st.w - 1; x++) {
    for (let y = 20; y < gs.worldSurface; y++) {
      if (x < 380) {
        if (rng.next() < palmL && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
      } else if (x > st.w - 380) {
        if (rng.next() < palmR && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] === 0) growPalmTree(st, rng, x, y);
      }
      growTree(st, rng, gs, x, y);
    }
    if (rng.int(0, 2) === 0) x++;                 // Next(3)==0 跳列
    if (rng.int(0, 3) === 0) x++;                 // Next(4)==0 跳列
  }
}

1)])) return false;\n  if (!GEM_GROUND.has(st.type[gi])) return false;\n  // 两侧至少一侧同基座（cs:30568-30571）\n  const fitSide = (dx: number) => st.inBounds(x + dx, num)\n    && st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, num)] && GEM_GROUND.has(st.type[st.idx(x + dx, num)]);\n  if (!fitSide(-1) && !fitSide(1)) return false;\n  const height = rng.int(7, 12);                       // Next(TreeHeightMin, TreeHeightMax+1)\n  const num4 = height + 4;                             // + TreeTopPaddingNeeded\n  // EmptyTileCheck(x-2, x+2, num-num4, num-1, 20)（cs:30580-30582）\n  if (x - height; i < num; i++) {\n    let num6 = rng.int(0, 9);\n    if (i === num - 1 || i === num - height) num6 = 0; // 顶/底强制直干\n    while (((num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) && flagL) || ((num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) && flagR)) num6 = rng.int(0, 9);\n    flagL = false; flagR = false;\n    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) flagL = true;\n    if (num6 === 6 || num6 === 7) flagR = true;\n    const num5 = rng.int(0, 2);\n    const [fx, fy] = TRUNK[num6][num5];\n    st.setTileSilent(x, i, treeTileType, fx, fy);\n    if (num6 === 5 || num6 === 7) {                    // 左枝（cs:30741-30764）\n      const k = rng.int(0, 2);\n      const [bfx, bfy] = rng.int(0, 2) < 2 ?   if (typeof sheet === 'number') V_ID[i] = sheet;

function hashGrids(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; liquid: Uint8Array; w: number; h: number }) {
  const n = st.w * st.h;

  runCavesPass(st, rng, gs); out.set('surfacecaves', hashGrids(st));
  return out;
}

One subtle bug in my tuft/base frame logic: in vanilla, the crown `num5` roll is a single Next(3) shared across the base-frame if/else chain (cs:30901: `num5 = genRand.Next(3)` once, used for flag4&&flag5 / flag4 / flag5 branches). My code rolls rng.int(0,2) separately for tR and tL placement — RNG stream differs from vanilla (vanilla rolls: Next(3) for tR frame, Next(3) for tL frame, then Next(3) for base frame — actually vanilla: `num5 = genRand.Next(3)` before `if (flag5)` (frame pick for right tuft), then another `num5 = genRand.Next(3)` before `if (flag4)` (left tuft), then another before the base if-chain — yes, vanilla rolls three times too (lines 30859, 30884, 30900). ✓ matches mine (tR roll, tL roll, base roll).

Order: vanilla does flag5 (right) first, then flag4 (left), then base. Mine: tR, tL, base ✓.

Base frame: vanilla `if (flag4 && flag5) fx=88; else if (flag4) fx=0; else if (flag5) fx=66` ✓ mine matches (tL&&tR→88, tL→0, tR→66).

Good. Now retry the orphan kill.
